# Knowledge ledger

Canonical index of technical claims about DS3's D3D11 reference renderer, engine
structure and native D3D12 backend. For current work, use [STATE](../.project/STATE.md)
and the [project index](../.project/INDEX.md); this ledger preserves historical
claims and does not define the active workstream. Historical evidence links may
point to intentionally removed inputs; see the [retention policy](../.project/EVIDENCE-RETENTION.json).

The 55 high-framerate timing claims (LTF/TRTF) moved to the Desktop folder
`DS3 Timing Fixes (LTF-TRTF)` on 2026-10-02 and came back on 2026-10-21 under
[timing/](../timing/IMPORT.md) (documents and decompilation; the raw captures stay in
that folder). Their numbers, **K-0010 to K-0064**, are not in the index below: they are in
[timing/knowledge/CLAIMS.md](../timing/knowledge/CLAIMS.md) (cited by I-0066's pacing sheets, for instance K-0013 to
K-0020 for the timing object `[0x14489DD10]` +0x354/+0x358); the numbers of the two files do not overlap. Prior repository
history is in git tag `archive/pre-cleanup-2026-10-02`.

## Status vocabulary

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `HYPOTHESIS` | Possible explanation; not demonstrated. |
| `OBSERVED` | Directly measured or witnessed fact, without overextending its interpretation. |
| `SUPPORTED` | Multiple elements converge, but the conclusion lacks sufficiently direct proof. |
| `CONFIRMED` | Direct proof or a sufficiently strong reproducible body of proof exists. |
| `REFUTED` | Evidence invalidated the claim; keep it visible to prevent recurrence. |

Reports (STATE, RESULT, studies) also mark a single interpretation `INFERRED`: reasoned from observations but not tested
directly. It is a note in a report, not a claim status: a claim that rests on it is at most `SUPPORTED` (repository audit,
2026-10-25).

## Adding a claim

Use a stable `K-NNNN` identifier. A short claim may live only in this table. Create `knowledge/claims/K-NNNN.md` when provenance, scope, experiments, or conflicts need detail. Every evidence reference must resolve to an evidence manifest entry, investigation result, or stable external source with provenance.

Changing a claim requires adding the new evidence and recording the status transition in its detail record. Do not delete a refuted influential claim.
